# PackItUp.com.pk — Full Site Corpus

## POLICY: Privacy Policy
URL: https://packitup.com.pk/policies/privacy-policy

Privacy policy
PRIVACY POLICY
Pack It Up
At Pack It Up, your privacy matters to us. This Privacy Policy explains how we collect, use, and protect your personal information when you visit or shop from www.packitup.com.pk.
By using our website, you agree to the practices described in this policy.
INFORMATION WE COLLECT
When you browse our website or place an order, we may collect information such as your name, phone number, email address, shipping and billing address, company name (if provided), order details, purchase history, IP address, device type, and browser information.
We do not store your payment card details. Payments are processed securely through trusted third‑party payment gateways.
HOW WE USE YOUR INFORMATION
We use your information to process and deliver orders, communicate order updates, provide customer support, improve our services, send promotional offers if you opt in, and protect our store from fraud.
SHOPIFY & PAYMENT PROCESSING
Our store is hosted on Shopify Inc., which provides the e‑commerce platform that allows us to sell our products online. Your data may be stored through Shopify’s secure servers, databases, and applications. Payment transactions are encrypted and handled according to industry standards.
SHARING YOUR INFORMATION
We do not sell or rent your personal information. It may only be shared with delivery partners, payment gateways, and service providers essential for operating our store.
COOKIES
We use cookies to remember your preferences, manage your cart, and understand website traffic. You can disable cookies in your browser settings if you choose.
DATA SECURITY
We take reasonable steps to protect your personal information. While no system is fully secure, we work hard to safeguard your data.
YOUR RIGHTS
You may request access, correction, or deletion of your personal information and opt out of marketing communications at any time.
THIRD‑PARTY LINKS
We are not responsible for the privacy practices of third‑party websites linked on our site.
POLICY UPDATES
We may update this Privacy Policy from time to time. Changes will be posted on this page.
CONTACT US
Address: M22 mezzanine floor, Kohinoor square, Kemball Road, Aram Bagh, Karachi, 74200, PK
Email:
packitup.khi@gmail.com
Website: www.packitup.com.pk

## POLICY: Refund, Return & Exchange Policy
URL: https://packitup.com.pk/policies/refund-policy

Refund policy
REFUND & RETURN POLICY
Pack It Up
At Pack It Up, we aim to keep our refund and return process clear and fair for both our customers and our business. Please read this policy carefully before placing an order.
NO REFUNDS ON CUSTOM & DISCOUNTED ORDERS
Refunds are not available for:
• Custom or made-to-order products
• Items purchased during sales or on discounted prices
These orders are final and cannot be refunded once confirmed.
CHANGE OF MIND RETURNS
We accept returns for change of mind under the following conditions:
• The return request must be made within 48 hours of delivery
• The product must be unused, unopened, and in original packaging
• The product must be in resalable condition
IMPORTANT:
• Change of mind returns are NOT accepted for fragile items
• Shipping charges are non-refundable
• Return shipping charges will be borne by the customer
RETURN PACKAGING RESPONSIBILITY
Customers returning items must ensure:
• Products are packed properly and securely
• If the original packaging is damaged, items must be returned in new, strong carton or protective packaging
Pack It Up will not be responsible if:
• The returned parcel is received in damaged condition
• The returned parcel is lost during return shipping
In such cases, returns or refunds will not be accepted. Video evidence will not be accepted for damage occurring during return shipping.
DAMAGED OR INCORRECT ITEMS (FROM OUR SIDE)
If you receive a damaged or incorrect product:
• Contact us within 24 hours of delivery
• Provide clear photos or video proof
• Product must not be used
After verification, we will offer a replacement or refund depending on availability.
RETURN EXCLUSIONS
Returns will not be accepted if:
• The product has been used, opened, or altered
• Original packaging is missing or damaged
• The item is fragile and the return is requested due to change of mind
• The item was a custom order or discounted product
REFUND PROCESSING
• Approved refunds will be processed to the original payment method
• Refund processing may take 5–7 working days after inspection
• Delivery charges are not refundable
ORDER REFUSAL & COURIER RETURNS
If an order is returned due to customer refusal, incorrect address, or unavailability at delivery, the order will not be eligible for a refund.
CONTACT US
Email: packitup.khi@gmail.com
Phone / WhatsApp: +92 322 2620169
Website: www.packitup.com.pk
Pack It Up reserves the right to update this policy at any time.

---

## POLICY: Shipping & Delivery Policy
URL: https://packitup.com.pk/policies/shipping-policy

Shipping policy
SHIPPING POLICY
Pack It Up
At Pack It Up, we aim to deliver your orders quickly and reliably. Please read our shipping policy below to understand delivery timelines and options.
DELIVERY LOCATIONS
We currently deliver across Pakistan.
KARACHI DELIVERIES
• Standard Delivery: 2–3 working days
• Express Delivery: Same-day delivery (extra charges apply)
Express delivery is available only within Karachi and subject to order confirmation and availability.
OUTSIDE KARACHI (OTHER CITIES)
• Standard Delivery: 3–5 working days
Delivery timelines may vary slightly due to weather conditions, public holidays, or unforeseen courier delays.
SHIPPING CHARGES
Shipping charges are calculated at checkout.
Express delivery charges are additional and depend on order size and location.
ORDER PROCESSING
Orders are usually processed within 24 hours after confirmation. Orders placed on weekends or public holidays will be processed on the next working day.
CONTACT & SUPPORT
If you have any questions regarding delivery or shipping, please contact us:
📞 Phone / WhatsApp: +923222620169
🌐 Website: www.packitup.com.pk
Pack It Up reserves the right to update this Shipping Policy at any time.

Frequently Asked Questions
Q: How many people does this bowl serve?
A: Typically 2-3 people for main dishes, or 1 very hungry person for a complete meal.
Q: Can I microwave food in this bowl?
A: Yes! Remove the PET lid first, then microwave the kraft bowl safely.
Q: Is this bowl leakproof?
A: The bowl has excellent grease and moisture resistance for solid foods and thick sauces. Not recommended for very liquidy soups.
Q: How eco-friendly is this packaging?
A: The kraft bowl is fully biodegradable and compostable. The PET lid is recyclable #1 plastic.
Q: What makes this "extra height" beneficial?
A: The 7.7cm height allows for more food capacity, better layering, and prevents spillage of generous portions.
